SCATTER-DERIVED SWEEP REPORT -- 2026-07-27

derived stats (ground truth vs algorithm mean HR per file) -- the piece that was missing
relative to full_expanded_sweep_ALL_combos.txt (which has per-tick totals but no per-band
half/double breakdown for all 256, and no scatter/per-file view at all).
Per-band half/double %% are computed on the MEAN-HR-per-file metric (not per-tick), so
these numbers will differ somewhat from full_expanded_sweep's own per-tick figures.
